     - Wishlist.
   - Product sharing on social media platforms.
   - Ratings and reviews for products.
   - Address management (e.g., creating/updating/deleting addresses).
   - Checkout and online advance payment.
   - Order management (history, tracking).
   - My account features for personalized user management.

2. **Admin Features**:
   - User authentication (login).
   - Ability to create/edit/delete:
     - Products,
     - Product categories/sub-categories.

     - Product catalog,
     - Orders,
     - Customers,
     - Shipping,
     - Payments,
     - Roles & permissions.
   - CMS page management.
   - Ratings and review moderation.
   - View and analyze statistics/reports.

#### Out-of-Scope:
- Not explicitly specified. Scope exclusions or limitations may need to be defined during project planning.

---

### Non-Functional Requirements

  - Handle up to 100 concurrent users.

# Use Cases and User Stories for E-commerce Platform

---

## **Use Case 1: Product Search**
### Title:
Product Search Functionality

### Description:
Enable customers to find products based on keywords, categories, or filters.

### Actors Involved:
- Customer  
- System  

### Preconditions:
1. The customer has accessed the e-commerce platform (logged-in or guest).  
2. The product catalog is available in the system.  

### Postconditions:
1. Relevant product results are displayed to the customer.  

### Main Flow:
1. The customer enters a search query (keyword, product name, or category).  
2. The system processes the query and searches the product database.  
3. The system displays a list of matching products to the customer.  
4. If filters are applied (e.g., price range, brand, availability), the system refines the search results accordingly.  
5. The customer selects a product to view more details.  

### Alternate Flow:
- If no results are found:
  1. The system displays a message: "No products found matching your query. Please try another search term."  

---

## **Use Case 2: Checkout Process**
### Title:
E-commerce Checkout

### Description:
Allow customers to complete a purchase by paying for items in their cart.

### Actors Involved:
- Customer  
- System  
- Payment Gateway  

### Preconditions:
1. The customer has added items to their cart.  
2. The customer is ready to complete their purchase.  

### Postconditions:
1. The order is successfully placed and a confirmation is sent.  

### Main Flow:
1. The customer reviews their cart and proceeds to checkout.  
2. The system prompts the customer to log in or continue as a guest.  
3. The customer enters shipping information.  
4. The customer selects a payment method.  
5. The system calculates taxes and shipping fees and displays the total cost.  
6. The customer confirms the order.  
7. The system sends payment details to the Payment Gateway.  
8. The Payment Gateway processes the payment and sends back confirmation.  
9. The system generates an order confirmation and sends it to the customer.  

### Alternate Flow:
- If payment fails:
  1. The system displays an error message and allows the customer to retry or use another payment method.  

---

## **Use Case 3: Admin Dashboard Management**
### Title:
Admin Dashboard Operations

### Description:
Provide administrators with a dashboard to manage products, orders, and user accounts.  

### Actors Involved:
- Admin  
- System  

### Preconditions:
1. The admin has valid credentials to access the dashboard.  

### Postconditions:
1. The admin successfully manages platform operations.  

### Main Flow:
1. The admin logs into the dashboard.  
2. The system displays a summary of platform statistics (e.g., total sales, new orders, user signups).  
3. The admin navigates through different sections (e.g., "Products," "Orders," "Users").  
4. The admin can perform actions such as:
   - Add, edit, or remove products.  
   - View and update order statuses (e.g., "Shipped," "Delivered").  
   - Manage user accounts (e.g., activating or deactivating accounts).  
5. The system updates changes in real-time and reflects them in the customer-facing system.  

### Alternate Flow:
- If an error occurs (e.g., invalid changes or system downtime):  
  1. The system displays an appropriate error message.  

---

# User Stories

---

### **Customer-Facing Stories**

#### **Search Products**  
- "As a customer, I want to search for products by name or category, so that I can quickly find what I need."  

**Acceptance Criteria:**  
1. Customers can enter search keywords into the search bar.  
2. Results are displayed based on relevance or applied filters.  
3. A "No Results Found" page is displayed for queries with no matches.  

#### **Filter Products**  
- "As a customer, I want to filter search results, so that I can narrow down products to match my preferences like price or brand."  

**Acceptance Criteria:**  
1. Filters for price, availability, and brand must be available.  
2. Filtered results must adjust dynamically.  

#### **Checkout**  
- "As a customer, I want to add my shipping and payment details during checkout, so that I can complete my purchase securely and efficiently."  

**Acceptance Criteria:**  
1. Customers can enter shipping information without errors.  
2. Customers can review and confirm their purchase details (items, total cost, taxes).  
3. Payment must integrate with at least two payment gateways.  

---

### **Admin-Facing Stories**

#### **Manage Products**  
- "As an admin, I want to add, edit, or delete product listings, so that I can keep the catalog up-to-date."  

**Acceptance Criteria:**  
1. Admins can enter product details (title, description, price, images).  
2. Updates to products reflect immediately on the platform.  

#### **View Orders**  
- "As an admin, I want to view and update order statuses, so that I can ensure successful delivery."  

**Acceptance Criteria:**  
1. Admins can access all order details in one place.  
2. Status updates like "Shipped" or "Delivered" are logged.  

#### **User Management**  
- "As an admin, I want to manage customer accounts, so that I can prevent misuse of the platform."  

**Acceptance Criteria:**  
1. Admins can activate or deactivate user accounts.

---------- ModelClientStreamingChunkEvent (glossary_agent) ----------
### Glossary of Terms for Ecommerce Platform

#### **Technical Terms**
- **API (Application Programming Interface):** A set of defined rules and protocols for building and interacting with software applications, enabling communication between different system components of the ecommerce platform.
- **Back-End System:** The server-side architecture responsible for database management, business logic processing, and integration with other systems.
- **CMS (Content Management System):** Software used to create, manage, and modify digital content, commonly for managing product information, blogs, and promotional content on the ecommerce platform.
- **Load Testing:** A type of software testing to measure system performance under specific conditions, such as handling multiple concurrent users.
- **Server Load:** The amount of processing power, memory usage, and bandwidth consumed by the ecommerce platform, crucial for ensuring stability during peak user activity.
- **Throughput:** The volume of data processed by the ecommerce system over a specific period, impacting the speed of customer transactions.
- **Response Time:** The time it takes the system to respond to a customer's action, such as adding a product to the cart or completing a payment.

#### **Business Terms**
- **Customer Journey:** The end-to-end process a customer follows from first interaction with the platform to completing a purchase and beyond, including post-sales support.
- **User Roles:** Distinct categories of users (e.g., customers, vendors, administrators) with access levels and responsibilities tailored to their needs and tasks on the platform.
- **Vendors:** Businesses or individuals who supply products listed for sale on the ecommerce platform.
- **Business Lines/Brands:** Specific segments or subsidiaries under the parent organization, offering unique products or services on the ecommerce platform.

#### **Domain-Specific Concepts**
- **Functional Requirements:** Specific functionalities the ecommerce platform must provide, such as user registration, product search, filtering, shopping cart, payment gateways, and order tracking.
- **Non-Functional Requirements:** Attributes related to the performance, reliability, and scalability of the platform, including consistent load handling, security protocols, and data integrity.
- **Products & Services:** The items or offerings available for purchase on the platform, categorized to enhance searchability and relevance.
- **Translations:** The adaptation of content, such as product descriptions and UI elements, into multiple languages to serve diverse geographic markets.
  
#### **Platform-Specific Terms**
- **LeanIX Metadata:** A framework for organizing and tracking system architecture and data flows in the ecommerce platform, maintaining integrity and scalability.
- **Migration Capability:** The ability to transfer platform operations to other infrastructures with minimal disruption, important for long-term adaptability.
- **User-Facing Systems:** The interface and features directly accessible to platform users, such as product catalogs, dashboards, and support tools.
- **Non-User-Facing Systems:** Back-office systems invisible to the end user but critical for operations, including inventory management, billing, and analytics.

#### **Features & Functionalities**
- **Real-Time Transactions:** The capability to process payments and order placement instantaneously, ensuring seamless customer experiences.
- **Shopping Cart:** A virtual basket where customers can collect and modify selected items prior to checkout and payment.
- **Search and Filtering:** Tools enabling customers to find products quickly by applying criteria such as category, price range, brand, and availability.
- **Performance Metrics:** Key indicators used to evaluate the platform's efficiency, such as site speed, uptime, and user satisfaction scores. 

**READY**

# Use Cases and User Stories for E-commerce Platform

---

## **Use Case 1: Product Search**
### Title:
Product Search Functionality

### Description:
Allow customers to search for products using keywords, categories, and filters.

### Actors Involved:
- Customer  
- System  

### Preconditions:
1. The product catalog is available and up-to-date in the system.  
2. The customer has accessed the platform (as a guest or logged-in user).  

### Postconditions:
1. Matching product results are displayed.  

### Main Flow:
1. The customer enters a keyword or selects a product category in the interface.  
2. The system retrieves and displays products that match the query.  
3. The system provides filtering options such as price range, ratings, or product availability.  
4. The customer applies filters to refine the search results.  
5. The system updates the product listing based on the selected filters.  

### Alternate Flow:
- If no product matches the query:
  1. The system displays a message stating "No products found for your search term."  
  2. The system suggests related products/categories if available.  

---

## **Use Case 2: Add to Cart**
### Title:
Add Items to Cart  

### Description:
Allow customers to add products to their cart for purchase consideration.

### Actors Involved:
- Customer  
- System  

### Preconditions:
1. The customer is browsing product details or the search results.  

### Postconditions:
1. The product is added to the customer’s cart with the correct quantity and options.  

### Main Flow:
1. The customer selects a product to view its details.  
2. The system displays product details, including price, available sizes, colors, and stock status.  
3. The customer selects attributes (e.g., size, color) and enters the quantity.  
4. The customer clicks the "Add to Cart" button.  
5. The system confirms that the item has been added to the cart and updates the cart summary.  

### Alternate Flow:
- If the selected quantity exceeds the available stock:  
  1. The system displays a message: "Only X items available in stock."  
  2. The customer adjusts the quantity or chooses an alternative product.  

---

## **Use Case 3: Checkout Process**
### Title:
Product Checkout  

### Description:
Enable customers to purchase the items in their cart through a payment gateway and finalize the order.

### Actors Involved:
- Customer  
- System  
- Payment Gateway  

### Preconditions:
1. The customer has added at least one item to the cart.  
2. The payment gateway is configured and functional.  

### Postconditions:
1. The order is placed successfully, and a confirmation email/notification is sent.  

### Main Flow:
1. The customer reviews the cart and proceeds to the checkout page.  
2. The system prompts the customer to log in or create an account (optional for guest checkout).  
3. The customer enters shipping details.  
4. The customer selects a preferred payment method.  
5. The system calculates applicable taxes and displays the final order total.  
6. The customer confirms the order.  
7. The system sends payment details to the Payment Gateway.  
8. The Payment Gateway processes the payment and sends a confirmation response.  
9. The system generates an order summary and sends the confirmation to the customer.  

### Alternate Flow:
- If the payment fails:
  1. The system notifies the customer of payment failure and allows retrying with another payment method.  

---

## **Use Case 4: Admin Dashboard Management**
### Title:
Manage Dashboard Operations  

### Description:
Enable administrators to monitor and manage platform activities such as user accounts, product catalog, and order statuses.

### Actors Involved:
- Admin  
- System  

### Preconditions:
1. The admin must have valid credentials.  

### Postconditions:
1. The admin successfully manages platform operations.  

### Main Flow:
1. The admin logs into the admin dashboard.  
2. The system displays the main dashboard with platform statistics (e.g., total sales, open orders).  
3. The admin selects a module:
   - **Products**: View, add, update, or delete product listings.  
   - **Orders**: View all orders and update their statuses.  
   - **Users**: Manage user roles, account statuses, or permissions.  
4. The system validates the admin’s actions and updates the database.  
5. The changes are reflected on the customer-facing platform.  

### Alternate Flow:
- If the admin performs an invalid action (e.g., entering incorrect data):  
  1. The system displays an error message and prompts the admin to correct the input.  

---

# User Stories

---

### **Customer-Facing Stories**

#### **Search Products**  
- "As a customer, I want to search for products by keyword, so that I can quickly find what I am looking for."  

**Acceptance Criteria:**  
1. Customers can enter keywords or select categories to initiate a search.  
2. The search results page displays products that match the input.  
3. Filters for price, category, brand, and ratings are available.  
4. Display a "No products found" message if applicable.  

#### **Add Items to Cart**  
- "As a customer, I want to add products to my cart, so that I can save items I intend to purchase."  

**Acceptance Criteria:**  
1. Customers can add products with selected attributes (e.g., size, color) to their cart.  
2. A confirmation message is displayed when a product is added to the cart.  
3. The cart reflects the updated items and quantities.  

#### **Checkout**  
- "As a customer, I want to enter shipping and payment details during checkout, so that I can complete my purchase."  

**Acceptance Criteria:**  
1. Customers can enter shipping details without logging in (guest checkout).  
2. Customers can select and use a payment method.  
3. Upon successful payment, an order summary is generated and sent.  

---

### **Admin-Facing Stories**

#### **Manage Products**  
- "As an admin, I want to add and update product details, so that I can keep the catalog relevant and accurate."  

**Acceptance Criteria:**  
1. Admins can enter product attributes such as name, price, description, and stock level.  
2. Updated products appear in the customer-facing store immediately.  
3. Admins receive feedback for any invalid product updates (e.g., missing critical details).  

#### **Manage Orders**  
- "As an admin, I want to update order statuses, so that customers can track their orders."  

**Acceptance Criteria:**  
1. Admins can view all orders sorted by status (e.g., Delivered, Processing).  
2. Admins can update order statuses with relevant timestamps.  

#### **Manage Users**  
- "As an admin, I want to suspend or activate customer accounts, so that I can handle policy violations or requests."  

**Acceptance Criteria:**  
1. Admins can access user account details in the dashboard.  
2. The customer selects attributes (e.g., size, color) and enters the quantity.  \n4. The customer clicks the "Add to Cart" button.  \n5. The system confirms that the item has been added to the cart and updates the cart summary.  \n\n### Alternate Flow:\n- If the selected quantity exceeds the available stock:  \n  1. The system displays a message: "Only X items available in stock."  \n  2. The customer adjusts the quantity or chooses an alternative product.  \n\n---\n\n## **Use Case 3: Checkout Process**\n### Title:\nProduct Checkout  \n\n### Description:\nEnable customers to purchase the items in their cart through a payment gateway and finalize the order.\n\n### Actors Involved:\n- Customer  \n- System  \n- Payment Gateway  \n\n### Preconditions:\n1. The customer has added at least one item to the cart.  \n2. The payment gateway is configured and functional.  \n\n### Postconditions:\n1. The order is placed successfully, and a confirmation email/notification is sent.  \n\n### Main Flow:\n1. The customer reviews the cart and proceeds to the checkout page.  \n2. The system prompts the customer to log in or create an account (optional for guest checkout).  \n3. The customer enters shipping details.  \n4. The customer selects a preferred payment method.  \n5. The system calculates applicable taxes and displays the final order total.  \n6. The customer confirms the order.  \n7. The system sends payment details to the Payment Gateway.  \n8. The Payment Gateway processes the payment and sends a confirmation response.  \n9. The system generates an order summary and sends the confirmation to the customer.  \n\n### Alternate Flow:\n- If the payment fails:\n  1. The system notifies the customer of payment failure and allows retrying with another payment method.  \n\n---\n\n## **Use Case 4: Admin Dashboard Management**\n### Title:\nManage Dashboard Operations  \n\n### Description:\nEnable administrators to monitor and manage platform activities such as user accounts, product catalog, and order statuses.\n\n### Actors Involved:\n- Admin  \n- System  \n\n### Preconditions:\n1. The admin must have valid credentials.  \n\n### Postconditions:\n1. The admin successfully manages platform operations.  \n\n### Main Flow:\n1. The admin logs into the admin dashboard.  \n2. The system displays the main dashboard with platform statistics (e.g., total sales, open orders).  \n3. The admin selects a module:\n   - **Products**: View, add, update, or delete product listings.  \n   - **Orders**: View all orders and update their statuses.  \n   - **Users**: Manage user roles, account statuses, or permissions.  \n4. The system validates the admin’s actions and updates the database.  \n5. The changes are reflected on the customer-facing platform.  \n\n### Alternate Flow:\n- If the admin performs an invalid action (e.g., entering incorrect data):  \n  1. The system displays an error message and prompts the admin to correct the input.  \n\n---\n\n# User Stories\n\n---\n\n### **Customer-Facing Stories**\n\n#### **Search Products**  \n- "As a customer, I want to search for products by keyword, so that I can quickly find what I am looking for."  \n\n**Acceptance Criteria:**  \n1. Customers can enter keywords or select categories to initiate a search.  \n2. The search results page displays products that match the input.  \n3. Filters for price, category, brand, and ratings are available.  \n4. Display a "No products found" message if applicable.  \n\n#### **Add Items to Cart**  \n- "As a customer, I want to add products to my cart, so that I can save items I intend to purchase."  \n\n**Acceptance Criteria:**  \n1. Customers can add products with selected attributes (e.g., size, color) to their cart.  \n2. A confirmation message is displayed when a product is added to the cart.  \n3. The cart reflects the updated items and quantities.  \n\n#### **Checkout**  \n- "As a customer, I want to enter shipping and payment details during checkout, so that I can complete my purchase."  \n\n**Acceptance Criteria:**  \n1. Customers can enter shipping details without logging in (guest checkout).  \n2. Customers can select and use a payment method.  \n3. Upon successful payment, an order summary is generated and sent.  \n\n---\n\n### **Admin-Facing Stories**\n\n#### **Manage Products**  \n- "As an admin, I want to add and update product details, so that I can keep the catalog relevant and accurate."  \n\n**Acceptance Criteria:**  \n1. Admins can enter product attributes such as name, price, description, and stock level.  \n2. Updated products appear in the customer-facing store immediately.  \n3. Admins receive feedback for any invalid product updates (e.g., missing critical details).  \n\n#### **Manage Orders**  \n- "As an admin, I want to update order statuses, so that customers can track their orders."  \n\n**Acceptance Criteria:**  \n1. Admins can view all orders sorted by status (e.g., Delivered, Processing).  \n2. Admins can update order statuses with relevant timestamps.  \n\n#### **Manage Users**  \n- "As an admin, I want to suspend or activate customer accounts, so that I can handle policy violations or requests."  \n\n**Acceptance Criteria:**  \n1. Admins can access user account details in the dashboard.  \n2. Actions like account deactivation or reactivation are logged in the system.  \n3. Customers receive a notification upon account changes.  \n\n---\n\n**READY**', name='use_case_agent', call_id='call_iuShiLZWh8q1zGOgOcbpbXcA', is_error=False)] type='ToolCallExecutionEvent'

Interface):** A set of defined rules and protocols for building and interacting with software applications, enabling communication between different system components of the ecommerce platform.\n- **Back-End System:** The server-side architecture responsible for database management, business logic processing, and integration with other systems.\n- **CMS (Content Management System):** Software used to create, manage, and modify digital content, commonly for managing product information, blogs, and promotional content on the ecommerce platform.\n- **Load Testing:** A type of software testing to measure system performance under specific conditions, such as handling multiple concurrent users.\n- **Server Load:** The amount of processing power, memory usage, and bandwidth consumed by the ecommerce platform, crucial for ensuring stability during peak user activity.\n- **Throughput:** The volume of data processed by the ecommerce system over a specific period, impacting the speed of customer transactions.\n- **Response Time:** The time it takes the system to respond to a customer's action, such as adding a product to the cart or completing a payment.\n\n#### **Business Terms**\n- **Customer Journey:** The end-to-end process a customer follows from first interaction with the platform to completing a purchase and beyond, including post-sales support.\n- **User Roles:** Distinct categories of users (e.g., customers, vendors, administrators) with access levels and responsibilities tailored to their needs and tasks on the platform.\n- **Vendors:** Businesses or individuals who supply products listed for sale on the ecommerce platform.\n- **Business Lines/Brands:** Specific segments or subsidiaries under the parent organization, offering unique products or services on the ecommerce platform.\n\n#### **Domain-Specific Concepts**\n- **Functional Requirements:** Specific functionalities the ecommerce platform must provide, such as user registration, product search, filtering, shopping cart, payment gateways, and order tracking.\n- **Non-Functional Requirements:** Attributes related to the performance, reliability, and scalability of the platform, including consistent load handling, security protocols, and data integrity.\n- **Products & Services:** The items or offerings available for purchase on the platform, categorized to enhance searchability and relevance.\n- **Translations:** The adaptation of content, such as product descriptions and UI elements, into multiple languages to serve diverse geographic markets.\n  \n#### **Platform-Specific Terms**\n- **LeanIX Metadata:** A framework for organizing and tracking system architecture and data flows in the ecommerce platform, maintaining integrity and scalability.\n- **Migration Capability:** The ability to transfer platform operations to other infrastructures with minimal disruption, important for long-term adaptability.\n- **User-Facing Systems:** The interface and features directly accessible to platform users, such as product catalogs, dashboards, and support tools.\n- **Non-User-Facing Systems:** Back-office systems invisible to the end user but critical for operations, including inventory management, billing, and analytics.\n\n#### **Features & Functionalities**\n- **Real-Time Transactions:** The capability to process payments and order placement instantaneously, ensuring seamless customer experiences.\n- **Shopping Cart:** A virtual basket where customers can collect and modify selected items prior to checkout and payment.\n- **Search and Filtering:** Tools enabling customers to find products quickly by applying criteria such as category, price range, brand, and availability.\n- **Performance Metrics:** Key indicators used to evaluate **Domain-Specific Concepts**\n- **Functional Requirements:** Specific functionalities the ecommerce platform must provide, such as user registration, product search, filtering, shopping cart, payment gateways, and order tracking.\n- **Non-Functional Requirements:** Attributes related to the performance, reliability, and scalability of the platform, including consistent load handling, security protocols, and data integrity.\n- **Products & Services:** The items or offerings available for purchase on the platform, categorized to enhance searchability and relevance.\n- **Translations:** The adaptation of content, such as product descriptions and UI elements, into multiple languages to serve diverse geographic markets.\n  \n#### **Platform-Specific Terms**\n- **LeanIX Metadata:** A framework for organizing and tracking system architecture and data flows in the ecommerce platform, maintaining integrity and scalability.\n- **Migration Capability:** The ability to transfer platform operations to other infrastructures with minimal disruption, important for long-term adaptability.\n- **User-Facing Systems:** The interface and features directly accessible to platform users, such as product catalogs, dashboards, and support tools.\n- **Non-User-Facing Systems:** Back-office systems invisible to the end user but critical for operations, including inventory management, billing, and analytics.\n\n#### **Features & Functionalities**\n- **Real-Time Transactions:** The capability to process payments and order placement instantaneously, ensuring seamless customer experiences.\n- **Shopping Cart:** A virtual basket where customers can collect and modify selected items prior to checkout and payment.\n- **Search and Filtering:** Tools enabling customers to find products quickly by applying criteria such as category, price range, brand, and availability.\n- **Performance Metrics:** Key indicators used to evaluate the platform's efficiency, such as site speed, uptime, and user satisfaction scores. \n\n**READY**", name='glossary_agent', call_id='call_pCZsZNHf9sNiBmpcnEUPzeaW', is_error=False), FunctionExecutionResult(content='use_case_agent: # Use Cases and User Stories for filters.\n\n### Actors Involved:\n- Customer  \n- System  \n\n### Preconditions:\n1. The product catalog is available and up-to-date in the system.  \n2. The customer has accessed the platform (as a guest or logged-in user).  \n\n### Postconditions:\n1. Matching product results are displayed.  \n\n### Main Flow:\n1. The customer enters a keyword or selects a product category in the interface.  \n2. The system retrieves and displays products that match the query.  \n3. The system provides filtering options such as price range, ratings, or product availability.  \n4. The customer is browsing product details or the search results.  \n\n### Postconditions:\n1. The product is added to the customer’s cart with the correct quantity and options.  \n\n### Main Flow:\n1. The customer selects a product to view its details.  \n2. The system displays product details, including price, available sizes, colors, and stock status.  \n3. The customer selects attributes (e.g., size, color) and enters the quantity.  \n4. The customer clicks the "Add to Cart" button.  \n5. The system confirms that the item has been added to the cart and updates the cart summary.  \n\n### Alternate Flow:\n- If the selected quantity exceeds the available stock:  \n  1. The system displays a message: "Only X items available in stock."  \n  2. The customer adjusts the quantity or chooses an alternative product.  \n\n---\n\n## **Use Case 3: Checkout Process**\n### Title:\nProduct Checkout  \n\n### Description:\nEnable customers to purchase the items in their cart through a payment gateway and finalize the order.\n\n### Actors Involved:\n- Customer  \n- System  \n- Payment Gateway  \n\n### Preconditions:\n1. The customer has added at least one item to the cart.  \n2. The payment gateway is configured and functional.  \n\n### Postconditions:\n1. The order is placed successfully, and a confirmation email/notification is sent.  \n\n### Main Flow:\n1. The customer reviews the cart and proceeds to the checkout page.  \n2. The system prompts the customer to log in or create an account (optional for guest checkout).  \n3. The customer enters shipping details.  \n4. The customer selects a preferred payment method.  \n5. The system calculates applicable taxes and displays the final order total.  \n6. The customer confirms the order.  \n7. The system sends payment details to the Payment Gateway.  \n8. The Payment Gateway processes the payment and sends a confirmation response.  \n9. The system generates an order summary and sends the confirmation to the customer.  \n\n### Alternate Flow:\n- If the payment fails:\n  1. The system notifies the customer of payment failure and allows retrying with another payment method.  \n\n---\n\n## **Use Case 4: Admin Dashboard Management**\n### Title:\nManage Dashboard Operations  \n\n### Description:\nEnable administrators to monitor and manage platform activities such as user accounts, product catalog, and order statuses.\n\n### Actors Involved:\n- Admin  \n- System  \n\n### Preconditions:\n1. The admin must have valid credentials.  \n\n### Postconditions:\n1. The admin successfully manages platform operations.  \n\n### Main Flow:\n1. The admin logs into the admin dashboard.  \n2. The system displays the main dashboard with platform statistics (e.g., total sales, open orders).  \n3. The admin selects a module:\n   - **Products**: View, add, update, or delete product listings.  \n   - **Orders**: View all orders and update their statuses.  \n   - **Users**: Manage user roles, account statuses, or permissions.  \n4. The system validates the admin’s actions and updates the database.  \n5. The changes are reflected on the customer-facing platform.  \n\n### Alternate Flow:\n- If the admin performs an invalid action (e.g., entering incorrect data):  \n  1. Upon successful payment, an order summary is generated and sent.  \n\n---\n\n### **Admin-Facing Stories**\n\n#### **Manage Products**  \n- "As an admin, I want to add and update product details, so that I can keep the catalog relevant and accurate."  \n\n**Acceptance Criteria:**  \n1. Admins can enter product attributes such as name, price, description, and stock level.  \n2. Updated products appear in the customer-facing store immediately.  \n3. Admins receive feedback for any invalid product updates (e.g., missing critical details).  \n\n#### **Manage Orders**  \n- "As an admin, I want to update order statuses, so that customers can track their orders."  \n\n**Acceptance Criteria:**  \n1. Admins can view all orders sorted by status (e.g., Delivered, Processing).  \n2. Admins can update order statuses with relevant timestamps.  \n\n#### **Manage Users**  \n- "As an admin, I want to suspend or activate customer accounts, so that I can handle policy violations or requests."  \n\n**Acceptance Criteria:**  \n1. Admins can access user account details in the dashboard.  \n2. Actions like account deactivation or reactivation are logged in the system.  \n3. # **Functional Specification Document**

---

## **1. Title Page**
**Project Name:** E-commerce Platform for Apparel  
**Version:** 1.0  
**Date:** [Insert Date]  

---

## **2. Introduction**

### **Overview**
The project entails creating an E-commerce platform for selling apparel online. Customers will utilize the frontend interface for product browsing and online order placements, with a secure payment mechanism. The backend enables admins to manage products, pricing, orders, and categories seamlessly.  

### **Objectives**
The platform aims to:  
1. Offer a seamless experience for customers placing online orders.
2. Provide efficient administration and management tools for the business owner.
3. Optimize scalability and performance for future growth.  

---

## **3. Scope**

### **In-Scope Functionalities**

#### **Customer Functionalities**  
- User Registration/Login  
- Comprehensive product search and filtering  
- Cart/Wishlist management  
- Checkout with online payments  
- Order history & tracking  
- Ratings/Reviews on products  
- Sharing products on social media  

#### **Admin Functionalities**
- Authentication/Role-based admin control  
- Inventory management for products and categories  
- Customer, shipping, order, and payment management  
- Content management (CMS)  
- Reporting and analytics  

### **Out-Of-Scope**
- Deferred exploration of loyalty or subscription programs.  

---

## **4. Functional Requirements**
1. Customers must be able to register, log in, and manage their profiles.  
2. The product search interface must enable keyword searches and filtering.  
3. Customer orders must be completed via integrated, secure payment gateways.  
4. Admins require tools to manage products, categories, and order tracking effectively.  

---

## **5. Non-Functional Requirements**

### **Performance** 
- Pages must load within 30 seconds over stable internet connections.  

### **Capacity**
- Scalability up to 100 concurrent users during peak processing.

### **Reliability**
- High uptime without broken or unavailable pages.  

### **Compliance**
- Security measures for data protection based on GDPR.  

---

## **6. User Roles & Personas**

- **Customer:** Shops apparel via the user-friendly web interface.  
- **Admin (Business Owner):** Handles backend operations using an Admin dashboard.  

---

## **7. Assumptions & Constraints**

1. Active internet connection is mandatory for processing.  
2. Project may exclude integrations for advanced AI/search functionalities.  

---

## **8. Use Cases**
*Embedded is the document generated via use_case_agent detailing Customer Flows & Admin Management Scenarios.*

The amount of processing power, memory usage, and bandwidth consumed by the ecommerce platform, crucial for ensuring stability during peak user activity.  \\n- **Throughput:** The volume of data processed by the ecommerce system over a specific period, impacting the speed of customer transactions.  \\n- **Response Time:** The time it takes the system to respond to a customer\'s action, such as adding a product to the cart or completing a payment.  \\n\\n### Business Terms\\n- **Customer Journey:** The end-to-end process a customer follows from first interaction with the platform to completing a purchase and beyond, including post-sales support.  \\n- **User Roles:** Distinct categories of users (e.g., customers, vendors, administrators) with access levels and responsibilities **Updated Summary of Requirements**

Here is the further updated ecommerce website requirements summary after incorporating additional details:

---

### **Product Overview**
- Development of an ecommerce platform for selling apparel.
- **Frontend Website**:
  - Customers can search for products, view product info, and place orders with secure online payment.
- **Backend/Admin Panel**:
  - Admins (business owners) manage the product catalog, categories, orders, and customer data.

---

### **KPI & Success Metrics**
- **Technical KPIs**:
  - Scalability: Support up to 100 concurrent users.
  - Performance: Page load times under 30 seconds.
  - Reliability: No broken links or "page not found" errors.
- **User/Business Metrics**: To be finalized in planning phases.

---

### **Scope (In and Out)**

#### In-Scope:
1. **Visitor Features**:
   - Product browsing.
   - View product ratings and reviews.
   - Social media sharing.
   - Check shipping availability.
2. **Customer/Buyer Features**:
   - Account functionality (registration, login, password management).
   - Product searching and filtering.
   - Wishlist and shopping cart.
   - Secure checkout and online payment.
   - Review orders and submit product ratings.
3. **Admin Features**:
   - Manage products, categories, orders, and payments.
   - Analytics/reporting tools.
   - CMS (Content Management System).
   - Manage customer accounts and permissions.

#### Constraints Summary (Updated):
- **Budget**: Fixed with strict adherence to project boundaries.
- Timeline: Delivery scheduled for **October 31st**.
---

### **Non-Functional Requirements**
- **Security**:
  - SSL encryption for payment transactions.
  - Secure coding practices aligning with GDPR requirements.
- **Performance**:
  - Handle 100 concurrent users and deliver a fast user experience (<30s page loads).
- **Usability**:
  - Intuitive UI for both buyers and admins.

---

### **User Roles**
1. **Visitors**:
   - Browse/search products, view product information, share products on social media, and contact support.
2. **Buyers**:
   - Full account capabilities, wishlist management, secure purchase functionality, and order tracking.
3. **Admins**:
   - Full operational management of the ecommerce platform, including role management, analytics, and CMS.

---

### **Integrations**
1. CMS for content updates.
2. Email Management tools.
3. Social Media Sharing.
4. Payments (specific provider TBD).

---

Would you like to add or clarify any information, or do you APPROVE the updated summary?  
If it's final, please reply with **APPROVE SUMMARY**.

**Finalized Summary of Requirements**

Incorporating the recent input regarding dependencies and risks, here is the finalized summary of requirements for the ecommerce platform:

---

### **Product Overview**
- Development of an ecommerce platform specializing in apparel sales.
- **Frontend Website**: For customers to browse products, place orders, and securely pay online.
- **Backend/Admin Panel**: For admins to manage products, categories, customer information, and orders.

---

### **KPI & Success Metrics**
- **Scalability**: Support 100 concurrent users.
- **Performance**: Pages should load in less than 30 seconds.
- **Reliability**: Ensure no broken pages or "page not found" issues.
- **Success Metrics**: Customer satisfaction and order completion rates (to be finalized).

---

### **Scope (In and Out)**

#### **In-Scope**
1. **Visitor Features**:
   - Product browsing, ratings/reviews, social sharing, check shipping availability.
2. **Buyer Features**:
   - Account functionality, search and filter products, wishlist, shopping cart, secure checkout, order tracking.
3. **Admin Features**:
   - Manage product catalog, orders, customers, roles/permissions.
   - Analytics/reporting tools, CMS functionality, customer management.

#### **Constraints**:
- Delivery timeline: **October 31st**.
- Fixed project budget; scope changes affect cost estimate.

---

### **Non-Functional Requirements**
- **Security**: SSL encryption for transactions, GDPR-compliant data protection.
- **Performance**: Handle 100 users simultaneously and load pages under 30 seconds.
- **Usability**: Intuitive user interfaces optimized for buyers and admins.

---

### **User Roles**
1. **Visitors**: Product browsing, social sharing, and shipping checking.
2. **Buyers**: Complete account and purchase management, including order history and wishlist.
3. **Admins**: Manage backend operations such as product inventory, orders, CMS, and customer data.

---

### **Integrations**
- Content Management System (CMS) tools.
- Payment Management (provider TBD).
- Social Media Sharing for product marketing.
- Email tools for communication.

---

### **Dependencies & Risks**
1. **Dependencies**:
   - Auditor approval of new processes.
   - Senior management buy-in on the approach.
   - Timely funding allocation.
2. **Risks**:
   - Lack of employee training regarding the system.
   - Developer resource shortfalls.

---

### **Mitigation Strategies**
- **Training**: Allocate resources for employee training on system usage pre-rollout.
- **Buy-In**: Regular updates to stakeholders to secure senior management support.
- **Resources/Funding**: Initiate early resource allocation and fallback plans for developer recruitment.

---

Would you like to add or clarify any information, or do you APPROVE the final summary?  
If it's final, please reply with **APPROVE SUMMARY**.

Use Cases\\n\\n### Use Case: Product Search  \\nEnable customers to search for products using keywords, categories, and filters. Matching product results are displayed. Error messages are shown for unmatchable queries.  \\n\\n### Use Case: Add to Cart  \\nCustomers can add items to their virtual carts, modify quantities, or adjust attributes before final purchase.  \\n\\n### Use Case: Checkout Process  \\nSecure online purchase; integrates payment gateways and ensures success notifications or retries for failed transactions.  \\n\\n### Use Case: Admin Dashboard Management  \\nAdmins manage products, orders, categories, users via dashboard modules; changes verified and logged accurately in the backend.  \\n\\n---\\n\\n## 10. Glossary\\n\\n### Technical Terms\\n- **CMS (Content Management System):** Software used to create, manage, and modify digital content like product catalogs and promotional information.  \\n- **SSL (Secure Socket Layer):** A standard security technology for establishing encrypted links between a web server and a browser, ensuring secure transactions.  \\n\\n### Domain-Specific Concepts\\n- **Functional Requirements:** Specific functionalities required by the users, such as user registration, product search, shopping cart, and payment gateways.  \\n- **Non-Functional Requirements:** Attributes related to system performance, reliability, scalability, usability, and security.  \\n\\n---  "}', name='write_md_file_tool')]

Title Page\n**Project Name:** E-commerce Platform for Apparel  \n**Version:** 1.2  \n**Date:** October 31, 2023  \n\n---\n\n## 2. Introduction\n\n### Overview\nThe project entails creating an E-commerce platform for selling apparel online. Customers will utilize the frontend interface for product browsing and online order placements, with a secure payment mechanism. The backend enables admins to manage products, pricing, orders, and categories seamlessly.  \n\n### Objectives\nThe platform aims to:  \n1. Offer a seamless experience for customers placing online orders.  \n2. Provide efficient administration and management tools for the business owner.  \n3. Optimize scalability and performance for future growth.  \n\n---\n\n## 3. Scope\n\n### In-Scope Functionalities\n\n#### Customer Functionalities  \n- User Registration/Login  \n- Comprehensive product search and filtering  \n- Cart/Wishlist management  \n- Secure checkout and online payment processing  \n- Order history tracking  \n- Ratings/Reviews on products  \n- Sharing products on social media platforms  \n\n#### Admin Functionalities\n- Role-based admin control for key operations  \n- Manage product inventory, orders, customer accounts, and shipping  \n- Reporting and statistics module with critical analytics  \n- CMS (Content Management System) functionality for updates  \n\n### Out-Of-Scope\n- Focus deferred on loyalty programs or subscription services.  \n\n---\n\n## 4. Functional Requirements\n1. Ensure visitor-accessible product browsing and keyword search.  \n2. Buyer review and order history functionality with wishlist control.  \n3. Admin-centric tools for inventory management and service tracking.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Non-Functional Requirements\n\n### Performance  \n- Capable of supporting up to 100 concurrent users. Pages must load under 30 seconds.  \n\n### Capacity  \n- Flexible backend implementation for system scalability to meet increased user volume.  \n\n### Security  \n- Adherence to GDPR standards for data protection. All online transactions must use SSL encryption.\n\n### Usability  \n- Interface will be designed with principles ensuring user-friendly navigation on both frontend and admin modules. Features include responsive design and accessible controls for users (buyers and admins).  \n\n---\n\n## 6. User Roles & Personas\n\n1. **Visitors**: Enable product browsing, social sharing, shipping availability checking, and accessing basic product information.  \n\n2. **Buyers**: Register and log in to search for products, manage wishlist/shopping cart, place orders, and review order history. Buyers can also provide reviews for their purchases.  \n\n3. **Admins**: Manage platform inventory, customer accounts, shipping workflows, oversee payments, and utilize CMS features to keep platform content up to date. Manage reporting and user roles/permissions. \n\n---\n\n## 7. Assumptions & Constraints\n\n### Assumptions\n1. The product inventory and physical storage are already established.  \n2. The admin will manage product catalog including SKUs.  \n3. Products offered via the platform will not include custom sizes or colors.  \n4. Orders will only accept payment in USD and will only cater to customers in the U.S. region.  \n\n### Constraints\n1. Budget and timeline constraints must strictly adhere to delivery goals (October 31, 2023).  \n2. New feature additions or changes may impact the project scope, testing, and deadlines.  \n3. Enterprise timelines for infrastructure updates will affect testing and delivery windows.  \n\n---\n\n## 8. Dependencies & Risks\n\n### Dependencies\n1. Approval from auditors and alignment with legal compliance.  \n2. Buy-in from senior management for project development and execution.  \n3. Timely allocation of funding.  \n\n### Risks\n1. Lack of employee training regarding system usage.  \n2. Challenges in obtaining developer resources within planned timelines.  \n3. Issues gaining stakeholder approvals (auditors and management).  \n\n### Mitigation Strategies\n1. Allocate sufficient training resources for employees before the rollout.  \n2. Develop fallback plans for recruiting surplus developer resources if required.  \n3. Regular project updates to stakeholders to address concerns promptly.  \n\n---\n\n## 9. Use Cases\n\n### Use Case: Product Search  \nEnable customers to search for products using keywords, categories, and filters. Matching product results are displayed. Error messages are shown for unmatchable queries.  \n\n### Use Case: Add to Cart  \nCustomers can add items to their virtual carts, modify quantities, or adjust attributes before final purchase.  \n\n### Use Case: Checkout Process  \nSecure online purchase; integrates payment gateways and ensures success notifications or retries for failed transactions.  \n\n### Use Case: Admin Dashboard Management  \nAdmins manage products, orders, categories, users via dashboard modules; changes verified and logged accurately in the backend.  \n\n---\n\n## 10. Glossary\n\n### Technical Terms\n- **CMS (Content Management System):** Software used to create, manage, and modify digital content like product catalogs and promotional information.  \n- **SSL (Secure Socket Layer):** A standard security technology for establishing encrypted links between a web server and a browser, ensuring secure transactions.  \n\n### Domain-Specific Concepts\n- **Functional Requirements:** Specific functionalities required by the users, such as user registration, product search, shopping cart, and payment gateways.  \n- **Non-Functional Requirements:** Attributes related to system performance, reliability, scalability, usability, and security.  \n\n---  ', name='read_md_file_tool', call_id='call_z6Y5tMXTEzXG2Pfy6xAzyrRU', is_error=False)] type='ToolCallExecutionEvent'

Functional Requirements\n1. Ensure visitor-accessible product browsing and keyword search.  \n2. Buyer review and order history functionality with wishlist control.  \n3. Admin-centric tools for inventory management and service tracking.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Non-Functional Requirements\n\n### Performance  \n- Capable of supporting up to 100 concurrent users. Pages must load under 30 seconds.  \n\n### Capacity  \n- Flexible backend implementation for system scalability to meet increased user volume.  \n\n### Security  \n- Adherence to GDPR standards for data protection. All online transactions must use SSL encryption.\n\n### Usability  \n- Interface will be designed with principles ensuring user-friendly navigation on both frontend and admin modules. Features include responsive design and accessible controls for users (buyers and admins).  \n\n---\n\n## 6. User Roles & Personas\n\n1. **Visitors**: Enable product browsing, social sharing, shipping availability checking, and accessing basic product information.  \n\n2. **Buyers**: Register and log in to search for products, manage wishlist/shopping cart, place orders, and review order history. Buyers can also provide reviews for their purchases.  \n\n3. **Admins**: Manage platform inventory, customer accounts, shipping workflows, oversee payments, and utilize CMS features to keep platform content up to date. Manage reporting and user roles/permissions. \n\n---\n\n## 7. Assumptions & Constraints\n\n### Assumptions\n1. The product inventory and physical storage are already established.  \n2. The admin will manage product catalog including SKUs.  \n3. Products offered via the platform will not include custom sizes or colors.  \n4. Orders will only accept payment in USD and will only cater to customers in the U.S. region.  \n\n### Constraints\n1. Budget and timeline constraints must strictly adhere to delivery goals (October 31, 2023).  \n2. New feature additions or changes may impact the project scope, testing, and deadlines.  \n3. Enterprise timelines for infrastructure updates will affect testing and delivery windows.  \n\n---\n\n## 8. Dependencies & Risks\n\n### Dependencies\n1. Approval from auditors and alignment with legal compliance.  \n2. Buy-in from senior management for project development and execution.  \n3. Timely allocation of funding.  \n\n### Risks\n1. Lack of employee training regarding system usage.  \n2. Challenges in obtaining developer resources within planned timelines.  \n3. Issues gaining stakeholder approvals (auditors and management).  \n\n### Mitigation Strategies\n1. Allocate sufficient training resources for employees before the rollout.  \n2. Develop fallback plans for recruiting surplus developer resources if required.  \n3. Regular project updates to stakeholders to address concerns promptly.  \n\n---\n\n## 9. Use Cases\n\n### Use Case: Product Search  \nEnable customers to search for products using keywords, categories, and filters. Matching product results are displayed. Error messages are shown for unmatchable queries.  \n\n### Use Case: Add to Cart  \nCustomers can add items to their virtual carts, modify quantities, or adjust attributes before final purchase.  \n\n### Use Case: Checkout Process  \nSecure online purchase; integrates payment gateways and ensures success notifications or retries for failed transactions.  \n\n### Use Case: Admin Dashboard Management  \nAdmins manage products, orders, categories, users via dashboard modules; changes verified and logged accurately in the backend.  \n\n---\n\n## 10. Glossary\n\n### Technical Terms\n- **CMS (Content Management System):** Software used to create, manage, and modify digital content like product catalogs and promotional information.  \n- **SSL (Secure Socket Layer):** A standard security technology for establishing encrypted links between a web server and a browser, ensuring secure transactions.  \n\n### Domain-Specific Concepts\n- **Functional Requirements:** Specific functionalities required by the users, such as user registration, product search, shopping cart, and payment gateways.  \n- **Non-Functional Requirements:** Attributes related to system performance, reliability, scalability, usability, and security.  \n\n---  ', name='read_md_file_tool', call_id='call_z6Y5tMXTEzXG2Pfy6xAzyrRU', is_error=False)]
